Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The adaptive variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 380 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 248 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_380(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 380. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^248(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the adaptive structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 404. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 251 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on adaptive structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 404. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 251. QED.
